We compare three post-training methods - supervised fine-tuning, reasoning-augmented fine-tuning (training on reasoning chains that justify a safety decision), and preference optimization (ORPO) - across three architecturally distinct models (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B, Qwen3-8B). We find that training method, not just data, reshapes how refusal is computed internally: reasoning-augmented training consistently produces a distinct kind of refusal computation, visible across all three models, while architecture independently shapes internal structure and how reliably refusal can be steered. Most importantly, no method we study achieves all three properties we would want from safe alignment at once: refusal that isn't concentrated in a few fragile components, safety gains that don't cost general capability, and safety behavior correctable through small, targeted edits. We caution against treating current post-training methods as a solved, reliable defense, especially for security-critical use. Code and models are available in https://github.com/hoangcuongnguyen2001/Beyond-Shallow-Alignment.

</details>


### [163] [CROCODIL: Cross-Model Code Editing with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03894)
*Linghan Zhong,Aditya Thimmaiah,Jayanth Srinivasa,Milos Gligoric,Junyi Jessy Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在编辑由其他模型生成的"外来代码"时的行为差异，发现模型倾向于对外来代码做出过多甚至过度的编辑，并提出CROCODIL后训练框架，通过相似性奖励与执行奖励的乘积来减少过度编辑同时保持功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发团队通常使用多个不同的LLM助手，不同开发者偏好不同模型，同一开发者也可能在不同会话中切换模型，导致一个模型生成的代码会被另一个模型编辑。由于各模型训练数据不同、风格偏好不同，需要探究LLM在编辑其他模型生成的代码时是否表现出不同的行为。

Method: 提出CROCODIL（Cross-model Code Editing with LLMs）后训练框架。其相似性奖励惩罚大幅度的代码改动，执行奖励根据构建和测试成功与否进行评分，将两个奖励的乘积作为优化目标，促使策略在减小编辑规模的同时不降低编辑任务成功率。

Result: 研究发现模型在编辑外来代码时倾向于产生更多且经常过度的编辑。CROCODIL框架通过相似性与执行奖励的联合优化，有效减少了过度编辑同时保持了功能正确性。代码已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了跨模型代码编辑场景中LLM的行为特征，证明了通过后训练框架可以调节模型编辑的"克制性"，在保持功能正确的前提下减少对外来代码的过度修改，从而提升多模型协作开发场景下的编辑效率与代码一致性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become ubiquitous tools for code generation and editing. However, development teams often use multiple LLM assistants. Different developers may prefer different models, and individual developers may switch between models across different coding sessions. Because of this, the edits any one model makes are frequently applied to foreign code originally generated by another model. These LLMs are often trained on different datasets, and as a result have different stylistic preferences. Do LLMs behave differently when they edit foreign code originally written by a different LLM with a different coding style? We find that models tend to make more, and often excessive, edits on foreign code. We introduce CROCODIL (Cross-model Code Editing with LLMs), a post-training framework for reducing excessive edits while preserving functional correctness. CROCODIL's similarity reward penalizes large changes, while its execution reward scores build and test success. We use the product of these two rewards to encourage the policy to decrease the edit size without decreasing the edit task success rate. CROCODIL is available at https://github.com/EngineeringSoftware/Crocodil.

</details>


### [164] [RuleMem: Active Rule Memory for Long-Term Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03915)
*Xingyuan Zeng,Zuohan Wu,Quanming Yao,Yue Wang,Wei Liu,Libin Zheng,Jiuke Wang,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RuleMem，一种基于规则的记忆框架，通过从长期对话历史中归纳可复用的逻辑规则，主动引导证据检索和推理，解决现有记忆机制被动存储导致的语义鸿沟和不可靠推理问题。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval两个基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话中的问答智能体需要处理海量且时间分散的对话历史，但现有记忆机制主要将过去信息视为被动存储的事实，导致语义鸿沟和推理不可靠的问题。

Method: RuleMem包含两个核心组件：(1)从对话中构建自然语言Horn子句作为可复用逻辑规则；(2)通过规则困惑度一致性（RPC）机制验证规则质量。这些归纳得到的规则既能检索语义距离较远的证据，又为答案生成提供显式的逻辑结构。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准上与14个基线对比，RuleMem取得最高准确率，超过基线平均值27.47个百分点（相对提升54.3%）。同时在LoCoMo和LongMemEval_s两个长期对话基准上进行了全面评估。

Conclusion: RuleMem证明了从对话历史中主动归纳逻辑规则而非被动存储事实，能显著提升长期对话问答的推理性能，是一种更有效的长期记忆机制设计范式。

Abstract: Question answering agents in long-term conversations must reason over massive, temporally dispersed dialogue histories. However, existing memory mechanisms primarily treat past information as \textit{passively} stored facts, leading to semantic gaps and unreliable reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose RuleMem, a rule-based memory framework that induces reusable logical rules from historical interactions to \textit{actively} guide both evidence retrieval and reasoning. Specifically, RuleMem constructs natural-language Horn clauses from conversations and validates them via a Rule Perplexity Consistency (RPC) mechanism. These induced rules enable the retrieval of semantically distant evidence while providing an explicit logical structure for answer generation. We conducted a comprehensive evaluation of RuleMem on two long-term conversational benchmarks, LoCoMo and LongMemEval_s*. In a rigorous comparison against 14 baselines on LoCoMo, RuleMem achieved the highest accuracy, exceeding the baseline average by 27.47 points (a 54.3% relative improvement).

</details>


### [165] [Fixed Suffix Dependency Ratio: Quantifying the Dual-Track Mechanism of Gender Assignment in Latvian Loanwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03930)
*Yelingyun Zhang,Atis Kapenieks,Marina Platonova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出固定后缀依赖比(FSDR)指标，通过分析1,832个拉脱维亚语名词词元类型，发现女性借词显著依赖固定派生后缀，而男性借词更多集中在自由选择区，从而为借词男性默认性别模式提供量化证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究反复观察到英语借词在不同接受语言中倾向于聚集在阳性/男性性别，但该模式的起源难以确定，因为固定形态规则和默认指派经常被混在一起分析，缺乏区分两者的量化框架。

Method: 提出固定后缀依赖比(FSDR)来量化不同性别对固定派生后缀的依赖程度，以区分形态锚定与自由选择；对拉脱维亚语1,832个名词词元类型进行分析。

Result: 结果揭示了借词系统内部显著的FSDR不对称性：阴性借词显著更依赖固定派生后缀，而阳性借词更集中于自由选择区域；该模式具有借词特异性，且在当代用法中更加显著。

Conclusion: FSDR为检验默认性别提供了量化框架，展示了阳性默认性别如何在语言接触中被激活并强化。

Abstract: Existing research has repeatedly observed the tendency for English loanwords to cluster in the masculine gender across different recipient languages, yet the origin of this pattern remains difficult to determine, as fixed morphological rules and default assignments are frequently analysed together. This study proposes the Fixed Suffix Dependency Ratio (FSDR) to quantify the degree of reliance on fixed derivational suffixes across different genders, and to distinguish between morphological anchoring and free-choice in distribution. By examining 1,832 Latvian noun lemma types, the results reveal a significant FSDR asymmetry within the loanword system: feminine loanwords rely significantly more on fixed derivational suffixes, while masculine loanwords are more concentrated in the free-choice zone. This pattern exhibits loanword specificity and has become more pronounced in contemporary usage. FSDR therefore provides a quantitative framework for testing default gender and shows how masculine default can be activated and reinforced under language contact.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Majority Vote: Multi-Perspective Adjudication for Medical Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03953)
*Joe Cecil,Marjorie Freedman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对聊天机器人长篇回复中的事实错误检测问题，提出多人多视角标注研究，发现单轮标注会漏检事实错误，而结合LLM作为评委、医学专家裁决和基于证据的事实核查的多轮裁决方法，虽能提高覆盖率，但标注者之间仍存在分歧，表明推理仍然依赖于判断、专业知识和证据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聊天机器人安全评估依赖于事实错误检测，但当前检测方法往往被简化为单轮、单标注者的问题。在长篇回复中，事实错误往往隐蔽地嵌入在基本正确的文本中，单轮标注容易遗漏，导致基于此类基准的评估可能低估错误率，影响安全性判断。

Method: 在医学相关的聊天机器人回复上设计了多视角标注研究，包括：（1）首轮标注（first-pass annotation）；（2）使用LLM作为评委进行候选错误发现；（3）两种裁决方式：医学专家裁决和基于证据的事实核查。此外将该技术应用于现有基准以验证通用性。

Result: 首轮标注者经常漏掉后来经裁决确认的事实错误；LLM作为评委能改善候选发现但单独使用仍不足，会漏掉标注者能捕捉的错误；不同裁决者之间存在分歧；将该方法应用于现有基准时也发现类似的漏注模式。

Conclusion: 单轮幻觉基准可能以牺牲事实错误覆盖率为代价获得规模优势；多轮裁决虽能提高覆盖率，但据此推断的结果仍对判断、专业知识和证据使用方式敏感，说明事实错误检测本质上是需要深度参与的判断过程。

Abstract: Understanding the frequency of factual errors in chatbot-generated text and evaluating systems that detect these errors is critical for determining chatbot safety. Yet factual-error detection is often treated as a single-pass, single-annotator labeling problem. In long-form chatbot responses, factual errors can be subtle and embedded within mostly correct text.
  We develop a multi-perspective annotation study of medically relevant chatbot responses, combining first-pass annotation, LLM-as-a-Judge (LaJ) candidate discovery, and two forms of adjudication: medical-expert and evidence-based fact-checking. First-pass annotators frequently miss factual errors later validated by adjudicators. LaJ improves candidate discovery, but is insufficient on its own: It misses factual errors that annotators catch. We also find disagreement among adjudicators, suggesting that adjudication over multiple candidate sources can improve benchmark completeness, but does not eliminate the need to apply judgment and expertise. Applied to an existing benchmark, this technique reveals a similar pattern of missing annotations. Together, these results suggest that in the settings examined here, single-pass hallucination benchmarks may achieve scale at the cost of undercounting factual errors. Multi-pass adjudication can improve coverage, but inferences drawn from the benchmarks are still sensitive to the judgment, expertise, and evidence used to determine error presence.

</details>


### [167] [Two-Stage Reinforcement Learning for Sound and Adversarial Test Generation in Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03955)
*Jiacheng Xu,Wentao Zhang,Zhiyi Lyu,Fuxiang Zhang,Chaojie Wang,Yang Liu,Bo An*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Test Cases Scaling (TCS)框架，将测试用例自动生成建模为对抗强化学习问题，通过两阶段RL训练测试生成器，使LLM能生成高质量测试用例来筛选代码生成结果，在TACO和LiveCodeBench基准上提升了pass@1和推理时答案选择性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成的强化学习依赖可执行反馈（测试用例），但高质量的测试用例稀缺——需要既正确（sound）又具有区分度（discriminative）。手动构造高质量测试用例成本高昂，因此需要研究利用模型自动生成测试用例的方法。

Method: 提出TCS两阶段强化学习框架，从滚动策略对齐缓冲区（rolling policy-aligned buffer）中训练测试生成器。阶段1：生成与参考解一致的测试（sound）；阶段2：将缓冲区限制在当前求解器的失败模式上，学习生成反例测试（discriminative/counterexample）。两阶段本质是形式化为对抗RL问题：模型针对求解器当前的失败模式生成有效的反例测试用例。

Result: 在TACO和LiveCodeBench两个基准上，TCS同时提升了pass@1以及根据生成测试进行的推理时答案选择性能。此外，学到的测试生成器也能有效用于筛选其他LLM的输出。

Conclusion: 测试用例自动生成可以被有效建模为对抗性强化学习问题，通过两阶段RL训练，学得的测试生成器不仅能提升代码生成模型的性能，还能泛化到对其他LLM输出的筛选，展示了以测试生成作为通用验证器/选择器的潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has substantially advanced code generation with large language models (LLMs) through executable feedback. The feedback for coding problems mainly comes from specific test cases, where high-quality test cases are often scarce since they should be both sound and discriminative. We thus turn to study the auto-generation of test cases using the learned model. We find this is naturally an adversarial RL problem: the model is expected to generate effective test cases as counterexamples, depending on the solver's current failure modes. We propose Test Cases Scaling (TCS), a two-stage RL framework for effective test generation. Both stages train a test generator from a rolling policy-aligned buffer: Stage 1 generates tests consistent with the reference solution, and Stage 2 restricts the buffer to current failure modes and learns counterexample tests. Across TACO and LiveCodeBench, TCS improves both pass@1 and inference-time answer selection according to generated tests. We find the learned test generator also enables effective selection among other LLM outputs.

</details>


### [168] [Investigating the Ability of Large Language Models to Analyze Recipes for Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03967)
*Revathy Venkataramanan,Aditya Luthra,Venkatesan Nadimuthu,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型(LLMs)分析食谱对糖尿病患者适宜性的能力。研究使用三种不同级别的提示词(直接查询、上下文引导、示例上下文)来测试模型能否检索并应用糖尿病饮食指南。基于7607个食谱(3807个适宜、3800个不适宜)的基准数据集，结果表明大多数LLM在判定食谱适宜性时趋于保守，而能基于饮食指南进行推理的模型表现更好，其中Mistral-7B和Llama 70B表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证实LLM在膳食规划方面具有积极作用，但尚未系统评估LLM分析给定食谱对糖尿病适宜性的能力。这一任务需要模型能够检索相关糖尿病饮食指南、将食谱分解为成分和烹饪方法并应用指南进行判断，构成了独特的多步推理挑战，值得专门研究。

Method: 研究设计了三种不同类型的提示词策略，融入不同级别的医学糖尿病饮食指南信息：(i)直接查询提示，仅依赖模型自身知识；(ii)上下文引导提示，提供相关饮食指南；(iii)示例上下文提示，通过示例进一步强化指南应用。同时构建了包含7607个食谱的基准数据集，其中3807个适宜糖尿病、3800个不适宜，用于系统评估多种LLM的性能。

Result: 实验结果显示，大多数LLM在预测食谱适宜性时表现保守，倾向于不判定为适宜以规避潜在不利后果。能够利用饮食指南进行推理的模型在预测糖尿病食谱适宜性方面表现更优。整体而言，Mistral-7B和Llama 70B相较于其他模型展现出更好的性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLM具备评估食谱对糖尿病适宜性的能力，但模型的保守倾向和指南推理能力是关键影响因素。上下文信息(饮食指南)的融入能显著提升模型表现，为未来在营养健康领域中安全可靠地应用LLM提供了重要洞见。

Abstract: Several studies have evaluated the ability of Large Language Models (LLMs) for meal planning, yielding positive outcomes. These models can process natural language inputs and leverage learned knowledge from their pretraining to generate meal plans. In this work, we investigate the ability of LLMs to analyze the suitability of given recipes for diabetes. The primary challenge for LLMs is to retrieve relevant dietary guidelines for diabetes, decompose recipes into ingredients and cooking methods, and apply these guidelines to determine the recipe's suitability. To study these challenges, we employ three kinds of prompts namely, (i) Direct Query Prompt (ii) Context-Guided Prompt, and (iii) Exemplary Context Prompt that incorporate different levels of diabetes dietary guidelines from medical sources. We introduce a benchmark dataset curated for this investigation consisting of 7607 recipes that include 3807 recipes suitable for diabetes and 3800 recipes not suitable for diabetes. Our results demonstrate that most LLMs are cautious in predicting recipes as suitable to prevent detrimental outcomes. Further, the models that can reason using the dietary guidelines performed better in predicting the suitability of recipes for diabetes. Overall, Mistral-7B and Llama 70B showed superior performance to their counterparts.

</details>


### [169] [Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox for Voice Dubbing and Full-Duplex Dialogue Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03992)
*Sanyuan Chen,Min-Jae Hwang,Sho Inoue,Anna Sun,Bokai Yu,David Kant,Dongmin Hyun,Dorian Desblancs,Gregory Antonovsky,Oleg Repin,Peng-Jen Chen,Xutai Ma,Zehai Tu,Juan Pino,Wei-Ning Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox (Text-AB)，一个基于Diffusion Transformer和flow-matching目标的统一框架，用于高质量语音配音与全双工对话合成。该系统采用DAC-VAE潜空间表示（将48kHz波形压缩为25Hz潜序列），去除了强制对齐与显式时长预测需求，并在480k小时语音上预训练了3B参数模型，随后在跨语言配音、全双工对话合成和情感对话合成三个下游任务上进行微调。实验表明其在配音基准上大幅超越现有系统，在对话合成上接近人类录音水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音合成系统（如Audiobox）在处理配音和全双工对话合成时面临三个主要瓶颈：一是音频表示压缩率不足、重建质量有限；二是依赖强制对齐和显式时长预测，流程繁琐且限制灵活性；三是模型规模和训练数据规模不足以支撑高质量的多任务生成。作者旨在构建一个统一且可扩展的框架，同时解决高压缩率音频表示、免对齐架构和规模化训练三大挑战。

Method: Text-AB采用Diffusion Transformer配合flow-matching目标，从三个方面区别于Audiobox：1）使用DAC-VAE特征在潜扩散框架中工作，将48kHz波形编码为25Hz潜序列，压缩率比EnCodec高10倍以上且重建质量更好；2）采用免对齐设计，直接通过现成的文本编码器消费原始文本，利用交叉注意力学习文本-语音对齐，消除对强制对齐和显式时长预测的依赖；3）大幅扩展模型与数据，预训练3B参数模型于480k小时单语语音，再通过监督微调适配三种下游任务。推理阶段支持约1分钟语音的一次性生成以及通过多扩散方案实现任意长时生成，并采用基于自动指标的多阶段重排策略提升质量。

Result: 在真实世界配音基准上，Text-AB相比最新的内部配音系统在韵律相似度、声音相似度、自然度和可分享性方面取得大幅改进；在全双工对话合成中，短对话上接近人类录音质量，长对话中在人类相似度和表现力方面大幅超越现有内部模型，并原生建模了轮流说话、反馈语和情感动态；在情感对话合成中，情感条件显著提升了情感对齐和情感交互质量。

Conclusion: Text-AB证明了免对齐的潜扩散语言模型框架结合规模化预训练能够同时胜任高质量配音和全双工对话合成任务，验证了DAC-VAE高压缩表示、交叉注意力免对齐机制以及多任务微调策略的有效性，为统一的语音生成系统提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: We present Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox (Text-AB), a unified framework for high-quality voice dubbing and full-duplex dialogue synthesis. Building on a Diffusion Transformer trained with a flow-matching objective, Text-AB departs from the Audiobox system along three dimensions. First, it operates in a latent diffusion framework using DAC-VAE features that encode 48 kHz waveforms into a 25 Hz latent sequence, giving over 10x higher compression than previous EnCodec representations while improving resynthesis quality. Second, Text-AB is alignment-free: it consumes raw text via an off-the-shelf text encoder and learns text-speech alignment through cross-attention, removing the need for forced alignment and explicit duration prediction. Third, we scale model and data substantially, pretraining a 3B-parameter model on 480k hours of monolingual speech, followed by supervised fine-tuning on three downstream tasks: cross-lingual voice dubbing, full-duplex dialogue synthesis, and emotional full-duplex dialogue synthesis. At inference, Text-AB supports one-shot generation for up to ~1 min of speech and arbitrarily long-form generation via a multi-diffusion scheme, plus a multi-stage reranking strategy that enhances quality based on automated metrics. On a real-world dubbing benchmark, Text-AB delivers a step-change improvement over the latest internal dubbing system, with large gains in prosody similarity, voice similarity, naturalness, and shareability. For full-duplex dialogue synthesis, it approaches human recordings on short-form conversations and substantially outperforms the latest internal model on long-form human-likeness and expressivity, while natively modeling turn-taking, back-channeling, and emotional dynamics. For emotional dialogue synthesis, emotion conditioning significantly improves emotion alignment and emotional interaction quality over the unconditioned baseline.

</details>


### [170] [Representational alignment yields generalizable safety in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04022)
*Lingyu Li,Yan Teng,Yingchun Wang,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，当前LLM中基于原型理论的道德概念分类结构保持较弱，并提出了表征相似性优化方法，直接对齐LLM潜在表征与人道德判断中的分类结构（而非监督生成响应）。结果表明，虽然标准行为对齐在响应层面学会了道德判断，但分类结构基本不变且对抗脆弱性增加；而重组道德分类虽在显式判断上提升有限，但能在多种基准和攻击策略下显著提升模型的对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM对齐方法主要优化可观察的响应，但当相同的恶意意图以人类能轻易识别的不熟悉或对抗性形式重新表达时，模型仍然脆弱。作者从原型理论出发，认为人类概念的适应性与围绕原型案例的分类结构有关，而当前LLM对此类道德概念分类结构的保持较弱，因此需要直接对齐表征层面的分类结构来提升泛化安全性。

Method: 作者首先在23个LLM上评估道德概念分类结构的保持程度，测量模型区分对立道德类别及类别内细粒度典型性的能力。随后开发了表征相似性优化（representational similarity optimization），该方法直接对齐LLM潜在表征与人类道德判断所表达的类别结构（使用251,334条道德标注），而不监督生成的响应。

Result: 研究发现23个LLM中许多模型无法区分对立的道德类别或保留类别内的细粒度典型性，且这些问题跨参数规模和对齐阶段持续存在。匹配实验中，标准的（行为级）对齐在响应层面学习了预期的道德判断，但分类结构基本未变且对抗评估中的脆弱性增加；而重组道德分类结构虽然对显式判断的改进较温和，但在跨模型规模、多样基准和攻击策略上持续提升了对抗鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 实验结果在功能层面支持了原型分类对行为适应性贡献的观点，同时表明将这一表征原则迁移到LLM中，可以实现在对抗条件下的泛化安全。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) is essential for their safe deployment. Current alignment methods mainly optimize observable responses, yet models remain vulnerable when the same harmful intent is recast in unfamiliar or adversarial forms that humans can easily recognize. Prototype theory offers an account of this adaptability. Human concepts are represented around central cases, and new instances are categorized according to their graded typicality relative to these prototypes. Here we show that such categorization of moral concepts is weakly preserved in current LLMs. Across 23 LLMs, models often failed to distinguish opposed moral categories or preserve fine-grained typicality within each category. These deficits persist across parameter sizes and alignment stages. We developed representational similarity optimization, which directly aligns the latent representations in LLMs with the categorization expressed in human moral judgements, without supervising generated responses. In matched experiments using the same 251,334 moral annotations, standard behavioral alignment learned the intended moral judgements at the response level while leaving the categorization structure largely unchanged and increasing vulnerability across adversarial evaluations. Reorganizing moral categorization produced more modest gains in explicit judgements but consistently improved adversarial robustness across model scales on diverse benchmarks and attack strategies. Our findings provide functional support for the view that prototype-based categorization contributes to behavioral adaptability. They also show that transferring this representational principle to LLMs yields generalizable safety under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [171] [Translation as a Decision Space: A Multi-Agent Perspective on Low-Resource Dialect Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04048)
*Hasan Alkhder,Mohammad Abboush,Igor Tchappi,Ahmet Zengin,Amro Najjar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将神经机器翻译重新定义为由自主翻译代理探索的结构化决策空间，以土耳其语-叙利亚阿拉伯语翻译为例，分析三种翻译路径（零样本直译、方言稳定化微调、英语枢轴翻译）的行为差异，并揭示多语言模型中的潜在决策灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NMT系统只产生单一输出，掩盖了多语言解码中隐含的替代决策路径。在低资源方言场景中，多个语言学上有效的实现可能在词汇真实性、语域和结构稳定性上有所不同，这种不透明性尤为突出。

Method: 将不同翻译路径建模为共享多语言骨干上的自主翻译代理，将代理间的分歧视为可解释的行为信号而非误差。设置了三个代理：零样本直译、轻量微调的方言稳定化翻译、以及通过英语的枢轴翻译。在5,000句对话数据上评估，并用5,000对额外语料（电视对话和MADAR-Turk资源）进行方言稳定化训练。不以传统性能指标为目标，而是量化方言标记频率、与标准阿拉伯语的词汇接近度以及结构方差的位移。

Result: 轻量级方言稳定化使方言标记使用率几乎翻倍（从0.2266增至0.4988），同时显著降低结构不稳定性。枢轴翻译引入标准化压力和可测量的压缩效应，零样本翻译呈现最高的决策方差。

Conclusion: 翻译代理间的分歧揭示了多语言模型内部的潜在决策灵活性，为低资源方言生成提供了一个可解释性框架，将发散性视为结构化的行为信号而非系统误差。

Abstract: Neural machine translation (NMT) systems typically produce a single output per input, obscuring the alternative decision trajectories implicitly available within multilingual decoding. This opacity becomes particularly problematic in low-resource dialect settings, where multiple linguistically valid realizations may differ in lexical authenticity, register, and structural stability. We propose reframing translation as a structured decision space explored by autonomous translation agents. Instead of analyzing a single output, we model distinct translation pathways as agents operating over a shared multilingual backbone. Inter-agent divergence is treated not as error but as an interpretable behavioral signal. We conduct an empirical study on Turkish--Syrian Arabic translation using three agents: (1) zero-shot direct translation, (2) dialect-stabilized translation via lightweight fine-tuning, and (3) pivot translation through English. Evaluation is performed on 5,000 dialogue sentences, while stabilization is trained on 5,000 additional Turkish--Syrian sentence pairs drawn from television dialogue and MADAR-Turk resources. Rather than optimizing for conventional performance metrics, we quantify structured behavioral displacement using dialect marker frequency, lexical proximity to standardized Arabic, and structural variance. Lightweight stabilization nearly doubles dialect marker usage, increasing it from 0.2266 to 0.4988, while significantly reducing structural instability. Pivot mediation introduces normalization pressure and measurable compression effects, whereas zero-shot translation exhibits the highest decision variance. We argue that translation divergence across agents reveals latent decision flexibility within multilingual models and we provide a principled interpretability framework for low-resource dialect generation.

</details>


### [172] [Sequential Beats Joint: On the Interplay between On-Policy Distillation and RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04108)
*Boyan Li,Bingsen Chen,Chenghao Yang,Ping Nie,Chen Zhao,Xi Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单的两阶段训练方案——OPD-then-RL（先在线策略蒸馏，后强化学习），在逻辑和数学推理基准上一致优于纯OPD、纯RLVR以及所有联合训练基线。作者通过pass@k行为、学习动态和参数更新提供了系统性解释：OPD扩展学生对教师支持解的覆盖范围，RL在该支持范围内锐化能力，而联合优化两个信号会导致干扰。并给出了实用配方：OPD验证分数是切换至RL的关键信号，OPD是比SFT更好的RL冷启动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的将RLVR和OPD结合的工作通常在同一训练步骤内融合两个信号（加权相加或教师调制重缩放RL优势），但这种方式可能导致两个信号相互干扰。作者旨在探究是否可以通过简单的时间分离（分阶段）来更有效地结合这两种训练方法。

Method: 提出两阶段训练方案：第一阶段使用OPD（在线策略蒸馏）训练，利用教师模型的密集token级监督；第二阶段切换到RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习），利用稀疏奖励进行强化。两者顺序执行而非联合优化，并系统分析了两阶段的训练行为与机理。

Result: 在两阶段方案在逻辑与数学推理基准上优于纯OPD、纯RLVR以及所有加权组合或教师调制重缩放等联合优化基线。机理分析表明：OPD阶段扩展学生对教师支持解的覆盖（广度），RL阶段在该支持下锐化输出质量（精度）；联合优化会使二者在梯度上产生干扰。OPD验证分数可作为切换到RL的最优时机信号，且OPD作为RL冷启动优于SFT。

Conclusion: OPD-then-RL是一种简单而强力的方法来组合RLVR和OPD，将两者从纠缠的信号转化为互补的训练阶段，可作为推理LLM后训练的有效实践配方。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have emerged as two dominant methods for post-training reasoning LLMs. Prior work uses OPD's dense token-level supervision to complement the sparse RL reward, fusing the two signals within a single step: either as a \emph{weighted-additive combination} or a \emph{teacher-modulated rescaling} of the RL advantage. In this paper, we show that a simple two-stage scheme, OPD-then-RL, consistently outperforms pure OPD, pure RLVR, and all such joint baselines across logic and math reasoning benchmarks. Beyond the empirical results, we further provide a systematic understanding of this through pass@$k$ behavior, learning dynamics, and parameter updates, yielding a consistent explanation: OPD expands the student's coverage of teacher-supported solutions and RL sharpens within that support, while jointly optimizing the two signals causes them to interfere.To provide a practical recipe, we find that the OPD validation score is the key signal for when to switch to RL, and that OPD is a better cold start for RL than SFT. Together, our results establish OPD-then-RL as a simple yet strong way to combine the two methods, turning two entangled signals into complementary stages.

</details>


### [173] [Last Translation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04173)
*Vilém Zouhar,Niyati Bafna,Mukund Choudhary,Maike Züfle,Sara Rajaee,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Sara Papi,Ona de Gibert,Bhavitvya Malik,Eliya Habba,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Patrícia Schmidtová,Michelle Wastl,Sheriff Issaka,Leshem Choshen,Stella Biderman,Antonis Anastasopoulos,Jan Niehues,Rico Sennrich,Mrinmaya Sachan,Ondřej Bojar,Kenton Murray,Jörg Tiedemann,Alham Fikri Aji,Philipp Koehn,Christof Monz,Alexandra Birch,Sowmya Vajjala,Chalamalasetti Kranti,Cristina España-Bonet,Nobin Sarwar,David Kaczér,Shunta Asano,Malik Marmonier,Daban Q. Jaff,Vaisakhi Mishra,Hend Al- Khalifa,Gabriele Sarti,Sourajit Saha,Nils Rehlinger,Juan Daniel Cuervo Villa,Jonathan Tonglet,Saugata Purkayastha,Dominik Macháček,Jagannathan Ramanujam,Heejin Do,Zuzana Nadova,Fred Philippy,Fabian Retkowski,Maria Lymperaiou,Silvia Casola,Hanna Yukhymenko,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Sangwon Ryu,Andrés Jerez,Ron Keinan,Shuaib Shuaib Yusuf,Avantica Vempati,Maria Carmen Staiano,Sukannya Purkayastha,Adrian Cosma,Vitalii Babenko,Erivan Inan,Aviral Nigam,Wafa Aissa,Fatima Haouari,Venkata Prasanth Kumar Gummadi,Mehdi Jafarzadeh,Valentin Scourneau,Lukas Edman,Kaiser Sun,Shaomu Tan,Mohammad Sadegh Gholizadeh,Johannes-Rudolf David,Dipankar Srirag,Javier García Gilabert,Ruta Binkyte,Manar Ali,Ana-Maria Bucur,Sabry E. Farrag,Youssef Saber,Yihong Liu,Jean Maillard,Cojocaru Nicoleta,Xiaochuang Yuan,Sina Ahmadi,Philipp Mondorf,Kaustubh Dhole,Roman Wixinger,Shenbin Qian,Manuel Tuor,Sergey Troshin,Jonathan Yahav,Fida Mohammad Thoker,Amir Arsalan Rezapour,Lance Calvin Lim Gamboa,Manon Reusens,Kätriin Kukk,Koel Dutta Chowdhury,Giuseppe Gallipoli,Christian Hoang,Shaswati Saha,Seth Aycock,Jan Kocoń,Bo Chen,Linh Vu,Vatsal Venkatkrishna,Arafat Ahsan,Luan Thanh Nguyen,Hassan Soliman,Daryna Dementieva,Theresia Veronika Rampisela,Ngoc Quynh Tram Do,Marius Huber,Kazuki Egashira,Azmine Toushik Wasi,Vladislav Poritski,Mike Zhang,Deep Shah,Paul Gavrikov,Luis Frentzen Salim,David Africa,R. Damanhuri,Bello Umar Bello,Anumit Garg,Gengyu Rao,Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi,Kamile Dementaviciute,Andrianos Michail,L D M S Sai Teja,Dawei Zhu,Yi Fan,Wei Liu,Farhan Farsi,Elias Herranen,Sankalan Pal Chowdhury,Karen Sanchez,Farzad Shami,Ashok Urlana,Zimu Wang,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Priyaranjan Pattnayak,Marii Ojastu,Hongbin Na,Emilian Radoi,Chenyi Zhao,Carlos Hinojosa,Andrea Gregor de Varda,Zaid Alyafeai,Reem Alzahrani,Nehal Kathrotia,Alex Flückiger,Ulysses Sekai Tully Carr,Jimson Paulo Layacan,Guy Kaplan,Ritwik Tiwari,Rishit Dagli,Oksana Volchek,Isaac R Caswell,Bowen Yi,Blanka Kövér,Amir Hossein Yari,Aicha Chorana,Zhengxiang Wang,Selja Keränen,Samuel Simko,Joy Olusanya,Jenny Chim,Enzo Doyen,Vivek Harsha Lakkamaneni,Sophia Conrad,Pouya Sadeghi,Panayiotis Panayiotou,Luis Lara,Jannatul Nayem,Eran Yahav,Debanshu Das,Antonia Karamolegkou,Anmol Goel,Aishik Mandal,Tommaso Cerruti,Raoyuan Zhao,Mykola Haltiuk,Thura Aung,Naser Almousa,Amir Hossein Kargaran,Rachel Bawden,Qiaoyuan Zheng,Mateusz Lango,Beni Egressy,Fidel Rodríguez Velásquez,Natchapon Jongwiriyanurak,Minh Ngoc Do,Marco Gaido,Lena Libon,Dzmitry Kuzmin,Badal Nyalang,Antoine Taroni,Andrei Niculae,Abdulaziz Nura Kani,Rushikesh Zawar,Marek Šuppa,Beatrice Savoldi,Andreas Simons,Rayyan Merchant,Ilai Yaron Levy,Francesco Pinto,Ziyi Yang,Yolanda Xavier,Samuel Frontull,Muhammad Ravi Shulthan Habibi,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Harris Abdul Majid,Francesca Padovani,Tim Graf,Tatiana Bielakova,Sharifa Djurabaeva,Shaoxiong Ji,Raia Abu Ahmad,Pavel Stepachev,Jirui Qi,Ayush Sunil Munot,Alireza Pakniat,Ayla Rigouts Terryn,Yuxing Lu,Yurii Paniv,Xiyan Fu,Tosin Adewumi,Sunisth Kumar,Stéphane J. P. S. Thunus,Shree Harsha Bokkahalli Satish,Shayan Bali,Prakhar Gupta,Papa Abdou Karim Karou Diallo,Matija Akrap,Marko Culjak,Kristýna Onderková,Joseph Attieh,Esrael Teferi Tensay,Elisabeth Fittschen,Benoît Sagot,Jingwei Ni,Yu Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"Last Translation Benchmark"(LTB)，一个由人工撰写并经同行评审的基准数据集，包含文本、图像、音频和视频等模态的翻译示例，用于打破现有最先进机器翻译模型的性能上限。同时引入基于手写验证规则的新型评估方法，实现可靠且可操作的失败案例分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译的标准基准正趋于饱和，自动翻译指标不可靠、易受奖励攻击且无法提供可操作的评估；即使是黄金人工评估也缺乏可复现性、客观性和可扩展性。这些因素阻碍了客观追踪翻译领域的进展及识别改进路径。

Method: 构建LTB数据集：收集人工撰写并经同行评审的示例（覆盖文本、图像、音频、视频等多模态），每个示例配有手写的验证规则，具体描述该示例上模型的具体失败案例，从而实现可靠且可操作的评估。数据集采用"活数据"模式，接受持续贡献，按时间节点发布版本（当前为LTBv1）。

Result: LTBv1是首个版本，包含2026年9月1日前被接受的贡献，未来将持续发布新版本以吸纳新收集的数据。

Conclusion: LTB通过持续更新的、突破现有模型极限的可靠基准及验证规则驱动的评估方法，为机器翻译领域提供客观、可操作且可复现的进展追踪工具。

Abstract: For scientific progress, we need benchmarks that test the limits of state-of-the-art models, and evaluation methods that inform us about failure cases. As models get stronger, standard benchmarks for machine translation are approaching saturation. Further, automatic translation metrics are unreliable, vulnerable to reward-hacking, and provide unactionable assessments. Even gold human evaluation is not problem-free, because it often lacks reproducibility, objectivity, and scalability. Overall, this prevents us from tracking objective progress in the field and identifying pathways for improvement. We introduce the Last Translation Benchmark, a collection of human-authored and peer-reviewed examples (texts, images, audio, videos) that break leading machine translation models. We also present a new evaluation approach: each example comes with handcrafted verification rules describing concrete failure cases on that example, therefore allowing reliable and actionable future evaluation. The Last Translation Benchmark is a live dataset that accepts ongoing contributions. The latest version is LTBv1, containing accepted contributions prior to September 1st 2026, with future releases planned as new data is continuously collected.

</details>


### [174] [Knowledge Acquisition During Pre-training? Large Language Models Learn Better With Auxiliary Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04180)
*Joseph Lee,Yidi Huang,Dokyoon Kim,Shu Yang,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究LLM在预训练中如何获取知识，提出辅助视角（对知识的重述）对学习具有因果帮助作用，并系统性验证了重复的必要性、辅助视角在固定token预算下优于文档重复、其效果不依赖教师模型强度、存在上下文性和基础性两类有助于填补知识缺口的知识形态，并从机制层面揭示层间偏差与压缩效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLM在预训练阶段如何获得知识仍存在理解空白。作者提出辅助视角（即知识的不同重述形式）可能对学习过程具有因果上的促进效应，希望通过受控实验来隔离并验证这一假设，从而解释数据多样性在预训练成功中的关键作用。

Method: 设计受控实验来隔离影响变量：(1) 确认重复是习得知识的必要条件，并阐明释义仅在较小批大小下才有帮助；(2) 在固定token预算下，将原本用于文档重复的token分配给辅助视图可改善学习效果，即使对于事实回忆任务也是如此；(3) 验证辅助视图的有效性不依赖于生成它们的教师模型强度；(4) 识别出有助于在先前知识缺口存在时促进学习的知识形态（上下文性与基础性知识）；(5) 通过层间偏差与压缩的视角探讨上述效应的机制性表现。

Result: 研究发现：重复是知识习得的必要条件；释义仅在较小批次下有助于学习；固定token预算下将重复token转用于辅助视图可意外地提升学习效果（包括事实回忆）；辅助视图效果与教师模型强度无关；识别出上下文性和基础性两类知识有助于填补知识缺口；机制层面揭示了层间偏差和压缩效应如何体现这些影响。

Conclusion: 研究表明，辅助知识表示（知识的不同重述形式）在大规模预训练语料中自然出现，是预训练成功的关键因素之一，为数据多样性为何重要提供了合理且可验证的解释。

Abstract: Gaps remain in our understanding of how large language models (LLMs) acquire knowledge during pre-training. We posit that auxiliary views, reformulations of knowledge, are causally helpful for learning. We design controlled experiments to isolate this. First, we confirm that repetition is necessary for acquisition and clarify that paraphrasing helps only at smaller batch sizes. Second, holding the token budget fixed, allocating tokens from document repetition to auxiliary views improves learning, counterintuitively, even for factual recall. Third, the effectiveness of auxiliary views is not contingent on the strength of the teacher model that generates them. Fourth, we identify forms of knowledge, contextual and foundational, that aid learning in the presence of prior knowledge gaps. Finally, we examine how these effects manifest mechanistically via layer-wise biases and compression. Together, our findings suggest that auxiliary representations of knowledge, which arise naturally in large pre-training corpora, are a key factor in the success of pre-training and offer a plausible explanation for why data diversity matters.

</details>


### [175] [Legibility is Not Interpretability: Comparing Judged and Actual Importance in Chain-Of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04194)
*Kevin Du,Alexander Hoyle,Laura Ruis,Acyr Locatelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过将推理步骤的重要性操作化为"优势值"（advantage，即该步骤对期望奖励/答案正确性的边际贡献，通过蒙特卡洛 rollout 估计），系统评估了 LLM 裁判能否从推理文本中识别高重要性的步骤。研究发现，足够强的 LLM 能超越流行度基线但远未达到噪声上限；将其微调为步骤级评论器对错误回答有显著提升，但对正确回答仍与上限有差距，表明步骤重要性仅能从推理文本中被部分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大量研究和实践将 chain-of-thought 模型的推理轨迹当作可解释的"窗口"，用 LLM 裁判进行错误诊断、忠实性评估以及通过过程奖励模型和生成式评论器进行步骤级监督。这些做法隐含假设推理步骤的文本内容编码了该步骤的功能作用（即是否重要），但该假设尚未被严格检验。作者旨在回应：推理文本是否真的编码了哪些步骤重要的信息？

Method: 1) 将步骤重要性操作化为优势值（advantage），即从推理轨迹中包含或排除某步骤时期望奖励（如正确答案产生概率）的变化，通过蒙特卡洛 rollout 进行估计作为代理真值；2) 基于这些估计作为 ground truth，评测 LLM 裁判识别高优势步骤的能力，对比流行度基线与噪声上限；3) 进一步将模型微调为步骤级评论器，分别针对正确回答与错误回答评估其性能。

Result: 1) 足够有能力的 LLM 裁判在识别高优势步骤方面可以超越流行度基线，但与噪声上限仍有明显差距；2) 经过微调的步骤级评论器在错误回答上有显著改进，但在正确回答上仍离噪声上限较远；3) 整体表明步骤重要性仅能从推理轨迹文本中被部分恢复（partially recoverable）。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹的表层可读性（legibility）并不能等同于可解释性（interpretability）。步骤重要性信号在文本中仅部分可恢复，这一发现对依赖推理文本进行过程奖励建模和步骤级监督的实践提出了警示，也为 chain-of-thought 忠实性研究提供了新的经验证据。

Abstract: Reasoning traces from chain-of-thought models appear to offer a legible window into how a model arrives at its answer. A growing body of work treats them as such, using LLM judges to diagnose errors, evaluate faithfulness, and provide step-level supervision via process reward models and generative critics. These practices rely on the text of a reasoning step carrying information about its functional role. But does the text actually encode information about which reasoning steps matter? We operationalize the importance of a reasoning step as its advantage: the change in expected reward, e.g., producing the correct final answer, from including that step, estimated via Monte Carlo rollouts. Basing ground truth on these estimates, we evaluate whether LLM judges can identify high-advantage steps and find that sufficiently capable LLMs can outperform a prevalence baseline but fall well short of a noise ceiling. Fine-tuning a model as a step-level critic yields strong improvement for incorrect responses but remains distant from ceiling for correct responses, suggesting that step importance is only partially recoverable from the text of the reasoning trace. Our findings contribute to a growing body of chain-of-thought faithfulness work that cautions against treating the legibility of reasoning traces as interpretability, especially with implications for process reward modeling.

</details>


### [176] [ESPO: Error-Structured Prompt Optimization via Diagnose, Diversify, and Stabilize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04197)
*Lihao Liu,Peng Tang,Kunwar Yashraj Singh,Shabnam Ghadar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ESPO，一种错误结构化的提示词优化方法，通过诊断-提出-选择三阶段解决进化提示优化器（如GEPA）的提示词膨胀问题。在7个基准上平均准确率比SOTA高3.76个百分点，提示词缩短47%，并在4个额外学生模型上均取得最佳平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化提示优化器存在提示词膨胀问题：每次迭代追加规则和注意事项，导致提示词长度增长达3倍但准确率并未提升。作者将其追溯为三个缺陷：不完整的错误观察、有限的搜索多样性以及不可靠的选择机制。

Method: 提出ESPO，将提示词优化分解为三个阶段：(1) 诊断——在一轮内将所有训练错误聚类为结构化模式；(2) 提出——通过四种互补策略生成候选方案，每种策略具有独立偏差；(3) 选择——应用bootstrap稳定性选择。泛化界（附录）将每个阶段与测试时间差距的对应项建立联系。

Result: 在7个NLP基准（Tweet, MMLU, GSM8K, HotpotQA, ScoNe, HoVer, PUPA）上，ESPO平均准确率比SOTA提高3.76个百分点（74.67% vs 70.91%），在每一个数据集上匹配或超越GEPA，同时提示词缩短47%（1,004 vs 1,878字符）且推理速度更快。在4个额外学生模型（Gemma 3 12B, Mistral 14B, Qwen3 32B, Claude Haiku 4.5）上均取得最佳平均准确率，最大提升出现在Qwen3 GSM8K（15.00%到91.40%）。消融实验证实关键预测：缺乏bootstrap选择而仅增加多样性反而损害性能（-1.20%）。

Conclusion: ESPO通过结构化的错误诊断、多样化的候选生成和稳定性选择，能有效缓解提示词膨胀问题，在准确率、提示词紧密度和跨模型泛化能力上全面超越现有方法。泛化界的理论框架为各阶段设计提供了支撑，消融实验验证了bootstrap选择对多样性策略的必要性。

Abstract: Evolutionary prompt optimizers such as GEPA suffer from prompt bloat: each iteration appends rules and caveats, producing prompts up to 3$\times$ longer yet no more accurate. We trace this to three deficiencies - incomplete error observation, limited search diversity, and unreliable selection - and propose ESPO (Error-Structured Prompt Optimization), which decomposes prompt optimization into three phases: Diagnose clusters all training errors into structural patterns in one round; Propose generates candidates via four complementary strategies with independent biases; Select applies bootstrap stability selection. On seven public NLP benchmarks - Tweet, MMLU, GSM8K, HotpotQA, ScoNe, HoVer, and PUPA - ESPO improves average accuracy by $+$3.76 pp over the state-of-the-art (74.67% vs 70.91% for GEPA), matching or exceeding GEPA on every dataset while producing prompts 47% shorter (1,004 vs 1,878 chars) and faster at inference. Cross-model experiments across four additional student models (Gemma 3 12B, Mistral 14B, Qwen3 32B, Claude Haiku 4.5) show ESPO yields the best average accuracy on every model tested, with the largest gap on Qwen3 GSM8K (15.00% $\to$ 91.40%). A generalization bound (Appendix) grounds each phase in a corresponding term of the test-time gap, and the ablation confirms a key prediction: adding diversity without bootstrap selection actually hurts performance ($-$1.20%).

</details>


### [177] [Compile by Training: Turning Natural-Language Specifications into Local Neural Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04199)
*Yuntian Deng,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"编译即训练"（compile by training）方法，将自然语言规范转化为可复用的神经函数：在编译时用教师模型生成任务特定样本训练小型适配器，运行时无需教师模型，可存储、版本化并像普通软件一样组合使用。在FuzzyBench-Hard子集上达到83.6%语义准确率，代价是编译时间约一分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多重复性文本函数易于描述但难以用规则实现，而每次调用大型远程模型会产生重复成本、延迟和对提供商的依赖，需要一种更经济高效的替代方案。

Method: 采用"编译即训练"框架：编译阶段由教师模型（大型模型）生成任务特定示例，用于训练部署在紧凑解释器上的小型适配器；生成的函数在运行时无需教师模型的参与，并可像普通软件一样存储、版本化和组合。

Result: 在FuzzyBench-Hard（Program-as-Weights快速编译器无法产生精确匹配的子集）上达到83.6%的语义准确率；相比快速编译器数秒的编译时间，该方法需要约一分钟的编译时间；编译器已部署于公开交互服务，并在多站点网站助手、语言控制的3D虚拟化身和双向英语-Claudish翻译器中展示了编译函数的应用。

Conclusion: 编译即训练在准确性与编译成本之间表现出权衡（更高准确率但更慢的编译），其生成的函数可作为可复用、可版本化的软件组件，在多种实际应用场景中展示出实用价值。

Abstract: Many recurring text functions are easy to describe but difficult to implement with rules, while calling a large remote model for every input introduces repeated cost, latency, and dependency on a provider. We present compile by training, which turns a natural-language specification into a reusable neural function. At compile time, teacher models generate task-specific examples that are used to train a small adapter for a compact interpreter. The resulting function runs without the teachers and can be stored, versioned, and composed like ordinary software. On FuzzyBench-Hard, a subset on which the Program-as-Weights fast compiler produced no exact matches, compile by training reaches 83.6% semantic accuracy. This higher accuracy comes with a higher compile-time cost: roughly a minute rather than seconds for the fast compiler. We deploy the compiler in a public interactive service and demonstrate compiled functions in a multi-site website helper, a language-controlled 3D avatar, and a bidirectional English-Claudish translator.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Structure and Implementation of New Practical English Textbooks Driven by Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02981)
*Ya Wang,Lei Zhang,Xueguang Yang,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI驱动的实用英语教材五层架构（知识图谱、学习者画像、任务生成、反馈编排、教师端治理），并在186名非英语专业大学生中进行了8周教学实验。相比静态数字教材，系统将单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%，口语任务平均分提高10.8分，教师批改时间减少31.6%，表明AI教材能在保持课程稳定性的同时提供个性化学习路径和可追踪的课堂数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统应用英语教材以固定纸质序列形式呈现，无法适应学习者个体差异。人工智能正在改变教材形态，使其成为能诊断学习者、推荐任务并提供形成性反馈的自适应学习系统，因此需要研究AI驱动的新型教材架构及其教学应用效果。

Method: 提出一个五层架构的AI驱动实用英语教材系统：知识映射层（knowledge mapping）、学习者画像层（learner profiling）、任务生成层（task generation）、反馈编排层（feedback orchestration）以及教师端治理层（teacher-side governance）。并开发原型系统，以186名非英语专业本科生为对象开展为期8周的教学实验。

Result: 相比传统静态数字教材：单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%（提升12.5个百分点）；口语任务平均分提高10.8分；教师批改时间减少31.6%。

Conclusion: AI驱动的教材能够在维持课程体系稳定性的前提下，提供个性化学习路径、丰富的练习材料以及可追溯的课堂数据，为数字教材向智能化方向演进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the form of applied English materials from fixed paper sequences to adaptive learning systems that can diagnose learners, recommend tasks, and provide formative feedback. This paper studies the structure and application of a new practical English textbook driven by artificial intelligence. A five-layer architecture is proposed: knowledge mapping, learner profiling, task generation, feedback orchestration, and teacher-side governance. A prototype was tested on 186 non-English-major undergraduates for eight weeks of teaching. Compared with a static digital textbook, the proposed system increased the unit completion accuracy from 72.4% to 84.9%, raised the average score for speaking tasks by 10.8 points, and reduced the teacher's correction time by 31.6%. Therefore, an AI-driven textbook can maintain the stability of the curriculum while providing personalised learning paths, rich practice materials and traceable classroom data.

</details>


### [179] [MasterControl Seventeen Every Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03209)
*MasterControl AI Lab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究一种受治理的企业分析框架：语言模型仅负责解释问题意图，确定性策略负责选择并执行预先审批的分析程序，程序同时返回结果和证据。该受限方法在关系运算、聚合、比较、窗口、排序和相似性构成的分析类内仍保持表达能力，且因固定语义、策略、数据和执行规则而具备可重放性。440次运行中，三个8B模型在运行时规划SQL并选择工具，仅Qwen3-8B解释意图并由策略执行审批程序。330次运行时规划均未在所有测试数据集上满足完整的"答案+证据"契约，而策略执行的分析器110/110全部匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析面临合规与可信的挑战：若让大语言模型在运行时自由规划并执行分析，难以保证结果的合法性、可审计性与可重放性。本文动机在于探索一种"受治理"的替代路径，在保持表达能力的同时，通过预审批程序与确定性策略来约束分析行为，从而同时满足准确性与治理要求。

Method: 采用两阶段架构：语言模型负责将用户问题解析为意图；确定性策略根据意图选择并运行预先审批过的分析程序。分析类限定为关系操作（含聚合、比较、窗口、排名、相似性），固定语义、策略、数据和执行规则以保证可重放性。实验对比了三种8B模型在运行时自行规划（生成SQL并选工具）与Qwen3-8B仅做意图解释、由策略执行审批程序的两种范式，共440次运行。

Result: 330次运行时规划（runtime planning）的尝试均未能在所有测试数据集上完成完整的"答案+证据"契约；相比之下，由确定性策略执行预审批程序的分析器在110次运行中110次全部达成契约。作者强调该结论是特定配置下的结果，不能据此推断所有运行时智能体在其他设计下都无法成功。

Conclusion: 受治理的企业分析范式——语言模型解释意图、确定性策略执行预审批程序——既能在定义的分析类内保持表达性，又能稳定满足"答案与证据"的合规契约。结果强有力但适用范围有限，不应泛化为对运行时规划范式的全盘否定。

Abstract: We study a governed approach to enterprise analytics: a language model interprets the question, while deterministic policy selects and runs a pre-approved analytical program that returns both results and evidence. We show that this restriction can remain expressive within a defined analytical class, using relational operations plus aggregation, comparison, windows, ranking, and similarity. Fixed meaning, policy, data, and execution rules also make results replayable. Across 440 runs, three 8B models generated SQL and selected tools at runtime, while Qwen3-8B interpreted intent only and policy executed the approved program. None of 330 runtime-planning episodes matched the full answer-and-evidence contract across all test datasets; the policy-executed analyzer matched 110 of 110. This is a configuration-specific result, not evidence that runtime agents cannot succeed under other designs.

</details>


### [180] [Speculative Macro Commit for Faster Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03236)
*Zeyu Liu,Souvik Kundu,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Speculative Macro Commit (SMC)，一种用于工具使用型LLM智能体的运行时加速机制。采用两层级智能体系统：大型权威actor模型生成官方轨迹，快速投机drafter模型在隔离环境快照上持续预测并执行未来动作链。SMC从事先挖掘的宏库中匹配动作链，当actor的下一步工具调用与drafter预测的首个动作匹配时，将预执行的草稿步骤及其观测一并提交到官方轨迹中，从而减少串行action-observation回合的等待时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型LLM智能体的延迟不仅来自模型推理，还来自串行的action-observation回合——每次工具调用、环境状态转换和观测返回都会延迟后续决策。为降低这类延迟，需要一种超越单步投机执行的多步复用机制。

Method: SMC构建两层级系统：(1) 大型权威actor模型产生官方轨迹；(2) 快速投机drafter模型在隔离环境快照上持续预测并执行未来动作链。SMC从训练迹线中挖掘重复出现的多动作骨架，存储到宏库中，用于运行时匹配drafter预测的动作链。当actor的下一步工具调用与drafter预测的首个动作匹配时，SMC将剩余预执行的草稿步骤及观测提交到官方轨迹。

Result: 使用Qwen3.5-27B INT4作为actor模型、Qwen3.5-4B作为drafter模型：在τ²-Bench Telecom子集上，SMC保持与串行智能体相当的准确率，同时比Speculative Actions (SA)基线降低10.23%延迟，比顺序执行降低18.59%延迟；在AppWorld上，比SA基线减少7.7%墙钟时间，比顺序执行减少44.9%，但任务完成率略有下降。

Conclusion: SMC提供了一种实用的方式来复用多步投机执行并降低智能体延迟，超越了单步投机动作带来的加速效果。代码已公开开源。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents spend wall-clock time not only on model inference but also in serial action--observation turns, where each tool call, environment transition, and observation can delay subsequent decisions. We introduce \textbf{Speculative Macro Commit} (SMC), a runtime mechanism for a two-tier agent system: a large authoritative actor model produces the official trajectory, while a faster speculative drafter model continuously predicts and executes future action chains on an isolated environment snapshot. SMC mines recurring multi-action skeletons from training traces and stores them in a macro library used to match against action chains predicted by the drafter at runtime. When the actor's next tool call matches the first drafted action, SMC commits the remaining pre-executed draft steps, together with their observations, to the official trajectory. Using Qwen3.5-27B INT4 as the authoritative actor model and Qwen3.5-4B as the speculative drafter model, SMC matches the sequential agent's overall accuracy while reducing latency by 10.23\% over the Speculative Actions (SA) baseline and 18.59\% over sequential execution on the $τ^2$-Bench Telecom subset. On AppWorld, SMC reduces wall time by 7.7\% over SA baseline and 44.9\% over sequential execution, with a small reduction in task completion. Overall, SMC provides a practical way to reuse multi-step speculative execution and reduce agent latency beyond single-step speculative actions. Our code is publicly available \href{https://github.com/zeyuliu1037/speculative-macro-commit}{\textcolor{magenta}{here}}.

</details>


### [181] [Fresh Memory, Stale Plans: Dependency-Scoped Validation for Distributed LLM-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03340)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PlanFence协议，解决分布式LLM智能体团队中的"陈旧计划执行"问题——即状态虽已更新但执行动作所依据的计划仍然过时的情形。PlanFence通过让计划引用其依据的具体公共记录，使执行者仅验证能影响待执行外部动作的相关记录，必要时重新规划或阻塞。在30个受控实时工作流中，仅依赖新鲜度的执行者在每个任务中都执行了过期计划，而PlanFence在无无效动作的前提下完成了所有任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLM智能体团队即使读取了最新共享事实，仍可能基于过时计划执行动作。例如，规划者从需求r3推导出动作，另一智能体提交r4，执行者收到r4后并未替换由r3生成的计划。这种"陈旧计划执行"问题表明，状态的新鲜性并不能保证授权动作的计划仍然有效，因此需要针对计划的依赖关系进行验证。

Method: PlanFence是一种依赖作用域的动作验证协议。具体而言：(1) 计划会明确引用其依据的精确公共记录；(2) 执行者仅验证那些可能影响待执行外部动作的记录；(3) 当验证不完整时，执行者选择重新规划或在验证完成前阻塞执行。该方法属于受控实验设计，而非通用任务准确率改进。

Result: 在30个经过计划修订的受控实时工作流中，仅依赖状态新鲜度的执行者在每个任务中都执行了过时计划，而PlanFence在无无效动作的情况下完成了所有任务。受控重放实验揭示了两个条件性边界：在低变更频率下，主动同步产生更低的协调停滞；而随着变更频率增长，PlanFence避免了重复的更新路径协调；随着共享键空间扩大，PlanFence避免了验证无关状态。

Conclusion: PlanFence为分布式LLM智能体团队中的计划失效问题提供了有效的安全防护机制。研究结果为受控的安全性和系统成本考量，而非通用任务准确率的提升。其适用性存在条件性边界：在低变更频率场景下，主动同步可能更优，而PlanFence的优势在变更频率高和键空间大的场景中更为显著。

Abstract: Distributed LLM-agent teams can read the latest shared facts and still act on an obsolete plan. A planner may derive an action from requirement $r_3$, another agent may commit $r_4$, and an executor may receive $r_4$ without replacing the plan derived from $r_3$. We call this \emph{stale-plan execution}: state freshness does not establish that the plan authorizing an action remains valid. We introduce PlanFence, a dependency-scoped action-validation protocol. Plans cite the exact public records they used, and an executor validates only the records that can affect the pending external action, replanning once or blocking when validation is incomplete. In 30 controlled live workflows with a post-plan revision, a freshness-only executor acts on the obsolete plan in every task, whereas PlanFence completes all tasks without an invalid action. Controlled replay reveals two conditional boundaries: proactive synchronization yields lower coordination stall at low churn, while PlanFence avoids repeated update-path coordination as churn grows and avoids validating unrelated state as the shared keyspace grows. These are controlled safety and systems-cost results, not general task-accuracy gains.

</details>


### [182] [A Prompt-Engineering Approach to Develop Scalable, Flexible, and Real-Time Hybrid Micro-Level Personalization in a General Purpose AI Teaching Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03402)
*Saptarshi Basu,Sandeep Kakar,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于提示工程的框架，用于个性化通用LLM/RAG驱动的AI教学助手（如Jill Watson），通过六个学习者维度产生96种学习者画像，并结合布鲁姆分类法分析查询认知复杂度，在不重新训练模型的情况下实现自适应教育支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI教学助手虽能提供可扩展的教育支持，但个性化能力有限。现有通用LLM教学助手在面对不同学科、课程和学习者时缺乏针对性的响应适配，需要一种无需模型重训练即可实现个性化的通用框架。

Method: 框架基于六个学习者特定维度构建个性化模型：自我评估、抽象偏好、冗长偏好、知觉取向、信息处理风格和理解水平，组合形成96种学习者画像。同时利用布鲁姆分类法估算学生查询的认知复杂度，并将学习者属性和认知评估编码为结构化提示词来调节LLM输出，无需模型重训练。

Result: 通过NLP指标的实验和五名参与者的人类研究进行评估。结果显示不同个性化条件下响应风格和结构存在可感知差异，统计分析识别出与可测量响应变化相关的学习者属性。

Conclusion: 研究提供了初步证据表明基于提示的个性化能够支持LLM驱动的教育代理实现自适应行为，为未来大规模个性化AI教育助手奠定了基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) teaching assistants powered by large language models (LLMs) offer scalable educational support but often provide limited personalization. This study presents a prompt-engineering-based framework for personalizing general-purpose LLM/RAG-based AI teaching assistants such as Jill Watson across academic disciplines and courses. The framework adapts responses using six learner-specific dimensions: self-assessment, abstraction preference, verbosity preference, perceptual orientation, information processing style, and level of understanding, yielding 96 distinct learner profiles. Student queries are additionally analyzed using Bloom's Taxonomy to estimate cognitive complexity at the interaction level. Learner attributes and cognitive assessments are encoded in structured prompts that condition the LLM without requiring model retraining. The framework is evaluated through experiments using NLP metrics and a human study with five participants. Results show perceived differences in response style and structure across personalization conditions, with statistical analyses identifying learner attributes associated with measurable response changes. These findings provide preliminary evidence that prompt-based personalization can support adaptive behavior in LLM-powered educational agents.

</details>


### [183] [Caught in the Story: Narrative Captivity in Multi-turn LLMs Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03407)
*Yuhe Wu,Guangyu Wang,Yujie Chen,Jiatong Zhang,Yuran Chen,Yutong Zhang,Xiyin Cheng,Wenpeng Cao,Zhuang Liu,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"叙事俘获"（narrative captivity）这一失效模式：LLM在多轮道德咨询中，面对无对抗性的一边倒叙述时，会将该单方描述视为完整信息并迎合叙述者的解释，而不寻求缺失的视角。作者构建了包含5,078个跨六种道德维度的人际冲突场景基准，在17个LLM上验证了这一现象普遍存在：多轮叙述下的最终判断比匹配的单轮基线平均偏移25个百分点。阶段级分析指出偏好优化是主要促成因素，四种推理时策略仅能部分缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM作为道德顾问的研究大多基于单轮判断或高压反驳的假设，与真实世界中人们寻求建议的方式不符。真实道德冲突对话往往包含一方自我辩护性的叙述，可跨多轮展开并造成信息不对称，但此前未见研究探讨仅靠叙述本身（无明确对立立场）能否在多轮咨询中改变模型判断。

Method: 1）构建包含5,078个人际冲突场景的基准，覆盖六个道德维度；2）设计匹配的单轮基线与多轮叙述实验对比；3）在17个LLM上评估"叙事俘获"现象；4）进行阶段级分析（如偏好优化阶段）归因主要贡献因素；5）测试四种推理时缓解策略。

Result: 多轮叙述下模型端态判断相对匹配的单轮基线平均偏移25个百分点，"叙事俘获"在17个LLM中普遍存在；阶段级分析显示偏好优化是主要贡献因素；四种推理时策略仅能部分缓解该问题。

Conclusion: LLM作为道德顾问时存在系统性的叙事俘获倾向，偏好优化（如RLHF）加剧了这一问题。现有推理时缓解措施不足，需要进一步开发能保持独立判断的LLM顾问系统。

Abstract: People increasingly turn to large language models (LLMs) for everyday advice, making ethically charged interpersonal problems a practical moral-advisory context. Most prior work has studied this context through single-turn judgments or pressure-laden rebuttals, assumptions that poorly match how guidance is sought in real-world contexts. These assumptions leave unclear whether narration alone, without an explicit opposing position, can shift model judgments during multi-turn moral consultation. Yet real-world moral-conflict conversation often elicits one party's self-justifying account, which can unfold over multiple turns and create information asymmetry. We introduce \textbf{narrative captivity}, a failure mode in which a model treats an unopposed one-sided account as complete and aligns with the narrator's interpretation without seeking missing perspectives. To measure this phenomenon, we build a benchmark of $5{,}078$ interpersonal-conflict scenarios spanning six moral dimensions. Across 17 LLMs, narrative captivity is widespread: end-state judgments under multi-turn narration shift by 25 percentage points on average beyond the matched single-turn baseline. Stage-level analysis identifies preference optimization as a major contributor, while four inference-time strategies provide only partial mitigation. We hope our project fosters LLM advisors that preserve independent judgment in real-world consultation.

</details>


### [184] [Dude: A Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for Paper-Code Discrepancy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03416)
*Weijie Liu,Running Zhao,Wenhao Yuan,Jinfeng Xu,Zhanfeng Xu,Xiaoxi Zhang,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dude，首个用于论文-代码不一致检测的双检测多代理系统。该系统通过粒度对齐协商和两阶段显著性过滤机制，解决了多代理设计中因论文语言与代码语言粒度不对称导致的过度解释和过度报告问题，在真实数据集上相比基线方法的召回率和精确率最高提升22.8%，F1分数最高提升18.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着科研论文提交数量超出人工审阅能力，LLM赋能的论文-代码不一致检测日益受到关注。然而，现有单代理LLM范式受限于上下文容量且仅进行单侧不一致检测，导致不一致检测的召回率表现不佳。

Method: Dude采用双检测多代理系统架构，核心包含两大机制：(1) 粒度对齐协商（Granularity-aligned Negotiation），解决论文自然语言与代码语言之间的粒度不对称问题；(2) 两阶段显著性过滤机制（Two-stage Salience Filtering），防止代理误报不一致，从而减少假阳性。

Result: 在真实世界论文-代码不一致数据集上的实验结果表明，Dude相比基线方法在召回率和精确率上最高提升22.8%，F1分数最高提升18.7%。

Conclusion: Dude是首个专门用于论文-代码不一致检测的双检测多代理系统，通过粒度对齐协商与显著性过滤有效缓解了多代理系统中的过度解释与过度报告问题，在检测性能上显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: LLM-empowered paper-code discrepancy detection has received growing concern since the scaling of research submissions exceeds the manual review capability. However, the limited context capacity and one-sided discrepancy detection of existing single-agent LLM paradigms lead to an inferior recall performance in detecting discrepancies. In this paper, we propose Dude, the first Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for paper-code discrepancy detection. We discover that the granularity asymmetry of the paper-language and code-language introduces over-interpretation and over-reporting challenges in a multi-agent system design for discrepancy detection, resulting in increasing false positives. To address this, we propose a granularity-aligned negotiation and a two-stage salience-filtering mechanism in Dude, which effectively prevents agents from falsely reporting discrepancies. Experimental results in real-world paper-code discrepancy datasets showcase Dude's significant recall and precision improvement by up to 22.8%, increasing F1 score by up to 18.7% compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [185] [DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval: Evaluating Implicit Instruction Following in Full-Duplex Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03423)
*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval（DSB-IFEval），一个用于评估实时语音对话系统中隐式指令跟随能力的基准，包含1,038个测试用例、八种助手角色和五种指令协议，并通过确定性指令遵循分数（IAS）和LLM评判的角色一致性分数（PAS）对六种实时语音系统进行评测，揭示了架构相关的行为权衡与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要通过显式轮次管理指令测试全双工语音代理的对话行为，而实际部署的代理往往通过角色或人设配置来推断适当的对话行为，缺乏对隐式指令跟随（即从角色/人设推断行为）的评测。

Method: 构建DSB-IFEval基准，包含1,038个测试用例和八种多样化助手角色，评估五种指令条件协议（默认行为、显式行为指令、人设隐含行为、人设与规则联合条件化、指令冲突）。采用两个评估指标：确定性指令遵循分数（IAS）衡量实时轮次管理，LLM评判的角色一致性分数（PAS）衡量人设一致内容。对六种实时语音系统进行系统评测。

Result: 发现架构相关的权衡：全双工模型（F-Actor和PersonaPlex）对指令是显式陈述还是需从人设推断较为敏感，人设-only条件下遵循度分别下降9.7%和4.5%；而GPT-Realtime、MiniCPM-o和Fun-Audio-Chat能强遵循人设一致内容，但其轮次管理行为在显式和人设-only指令间不变化，且在若干主动行为上受限。此外，系统即使能可靠遵循冲突指令到其规定人设，在安全冲突下仍难以覆盖这些指令。

Conclusion: 从角色推断适当行为、在合适的对话时机执行该行为以及解决竞争性指令，仍是全双工语音代理面临的三个截然不同的挑战。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents must continuously decide when to listen, backchannel, interrupt, handle speech overlaps, take the floor, and yield. Existing benchmarks largely test these behaviors through explicit turn-management instructions, while deployed agents are often configured through roles or personas from which the appropriate conversational behavior must be inferred. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval) for evaluating implicit instruction-following in real-time spoken interaction. (DSB-IFEval) comprises 1,038 test cases spanning eight diverse assistant roles and evaluates five conditioning protocols for instruction-following: default behavior, explicit behavioral instructions, persona-implied behavior, combined persona--rule conditioning, and instruction conflict. We measure real-time floor management using a deterministic Instruction Adherence Score (IAS) and persona-consistent content using LLM-judged Persona Adherence Score (PAS). Across six real-time speech systems, we find architecture-dependent trade-offs. Full duplex models like F-Actor and PersonaPlex are more sensitive to whether conversational behavior is stated explicitly or must be inferred from a persona, with adherence dropping by 9.7% and 4.5%, respectively, under persona-only conditioning. In contrast, GPT-Realtime, MiniCPM-o, and Fun-Audio-Chat strongly adhere to persona-consistent content, but their floor behavior does not adapt across explicit and persona-only instructions and remains constrained on several proactive actions. We further find that even if systems reliably follow conflicting directives to their prescribed persona, they still struggle to override them under safety conflict. These results show that inferring the behavior implied by a role, executing it at the appropriate conversational moment, and resolving competing instructions remain distinct challenges for full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [186] [Do GUI Agents Know When Not to Act? Enabling Conflict-Aware Termination for Multimodal GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03438)
*Zhaoyuan Huang,Tianjie Ju,Pengzhou Cheng,Zheng Wu,Yansi Li,Chuanbiao Song,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CONFLICTGUI基准和CONFLICTGUARD框架，解决GUI智能体在执行不可行指令时过度服从的问题。CONFLICTGUI覆盖指令内部冲突和指令-GUI上下文冲突两类场景；CONFLICTGUARD通过可行性验证协议和条件动作调制机制，在推理阶段抑制盲目执行行为。实验表明该方法在五个主流智能体上显著提升冲突任务成功率，同时保持正常GUI任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实用户在GUI操作中可能因无意的错误发出不可行（infeasible）指令，而现有GUI智能体对于"何时不应行动"缺乏判断能力，存在严重的执行偏向性过度服从问题——即使面对冲突指令也会继续盲目执行。

Method: 提出CONFLICTGUARD推理时框架，包含两个耦合组件：(1) 可行性验证协议，引导智能体在行动前评估指令逻辑和GUI侧证据；(2) 条件动作调制机制，将过度服从的执行行为转向终止导向行为。同时构建CONFLICTGUI基准，涵盖指令内部冲突与指令-GUI上下文冲突两类场景。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的GUI智能体上进行实验，CONFLICTGUARD显著提升了平均冲突任务成功率，同时保持了正常GUI任务性能不受影响。

Conclusion: 轻量级的推理时干预能够显著提升GUI智能体识别不恰当执行场景并克制不必要行动的能力，验证了将可行性意识与动作生成对齐的有效性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are increasingly used to execute natural-language instructions on user interfaces, yet real users may issue infeasible instructions due to benign mistakes. A reliable agent should not only know how to act, but also when not to act. In this work, we introduce CONFLICTGUI, a benchmark covering instruction-internal conflicts and instruction-GUI context conflicts to study conflict-aware termination. Our evaluation reveals severe execution-biased overcompliance: agents that perform well on feasible tasks often continue to execute blindly under conflicting instructions. To mitigate this behavior, we propose CONFLICTGUARD, an inference-time framework that aligns an agent's feasibility awareness with its action generation. CONFLICTGUARD contains two coupled components: a feasibility verification protocol that guides the agent to assess instruction logic and GUI-side evidence before acting, and a conditional action modulation mechanism that steers agents from over-compliant execution into termination-oriented behavior. Experiments across five widely-used agents demonstrate that CONFLICTGUARD improves average conflict task success rate significantly, while preserving normal GUI-task performance. These results validate that a lightweight inference-time intervention can substantially boost GUI Agent's competence to identify inappropriate execution scenarios and refrain from unnecessary actions.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond "Made with AI": Visualizing Provenance Density to Mitigate the Transparency Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03460)
*Qing Zhang,Yifei Huang,Juyoung Lee,Thad Starner,Jun Rekimoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"流畅性陷阱"（Fluency Trap）：当AI能轻易生成流畅文本时，用户不能再将流畅性当作真实性的代理指标。作者提议"Provenance Density"（溯源密度）——一种可视化证据密度（已验证主张在文本中的比例）的界面。81人用户研究表明该理想界面能显著区分真实与虚构内容（+4.15分，d=1.82），而无信号组无法辨别。200样本技术审计发现仅靠检索密度不够，关键判别信号来自"一致性否决"（Consistency Veto）。结论：AI内容与人类写作难以区分时，透明度应从作者身份披露转向证据可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使流畅的散文制作变得廉价，用户无法再将语言流畅度作为判断内容真实性的依据。现有的"Made with AI"二元标签仅披露作者身份，未展示支撑主张的证据，无法有效帮助用户识别AI幻觉或纠正对AI生成内容的系统性不信任。

Method: 提出Provenance Density证据可视化界面，展示文本中已验证主张的密度。通过两项实证研究验证：(1) 用户研究（81名参与者）比较理想化的Provenance Density界面与无信号条件对真实/捏造内容的辨别能力；(2) 200样本技术审计评估检索密度与一致性否决机制在不同查询类型下的判别有效性。

Result: 用户研究中，Provenance Density界面产生了较大的真实与捏造内容辨别差距（+4.15点，Cohen's d=1.82），而无信号参与者无法检测到可辨别的差异。技术审计意外发现，纯粹检索密度不足以区分；在动态查询中，一致性否决（Consistency Veto）承担了大部分判别信号。

Conclusion: 随着AI生成内容与人类写作越发难以区分，有效的透明度机制必须从单纯披露作者身份转向展示支撑主张的证据可视化。溯源密度提供了一种有前景的方向，其判别能力主要依赖一致性校验而非简单的来源检索。

Abstract: As generative AI makes polished prose cheap to produce, users can no longer rely on fluency as a proxy for truth. We call this failure mode the Fluency Trap: users trust fluent hallucinations while also discounting accurate content once it is disclosed as AI-generated. Binary ``Made with AI'' labels respond with authorship disclosure, but they do not show what supports a claim. We propose Provenance Density, an evidence-visualization interface that shows the density of verified claims in a text. In a user study with 81 participants, an idealized Provenance Density interface produced a large discernment gap between truth and fabrication ($+4.15$ points, $d=1.82$), whereas participants given no signal showed no detectable discrimination. A technical audit with 200 samples shows that retrieval density alone is insufficient; unexpectedly, the Consistency Veto carries most of the discriminative signal on dynamic queries. As AI-generated content becomes indistinguishable from human writing, effective transparency must move from authorship disclosure toward evidence visualization.

</details>


### [188] [AutoGraphForge: Towards Automated Graph Theory Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03478)
*Ján Pastorek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍AutoGraphForge，一个用于自动图论猜想-反驳-形式化-证明的计算流水线系统。该系统采用反例引导的多轮猜想生成机制，辅以新颖性过滤器（含559条经典关系）和约34.8万张图的反驳数据集，最终产出6,522条幸存猜想，并手工作证了其中非平凡关系。随后通过确定性翻译将猜想转为Lean 4语句骨架，并由两个神经证明器（DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B和OProver-32B）配合内核验证进行自动证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化数学发现长期以来面临两个主要挑战：一是如何高效生成有意义且非平凡的新猜想（避免重复已知结果和无意义关系），二是如何将发现的结果形式化并机器验证，实现从猜想生成到证明的端到端自动化闭环。

Method: 系统采用反例引导的多轮迭代：Graffiti3生成器在小规模快照表上逐步提出猜想，仅通过自身反例扩充表格；新颖性过滤器用559条经典关系经传递闭包和线性规划判定候选是否已被已知结果蕴含；幸存候选在约34.8万张图的综合数据集（含House of Graphs、所有连通图（≤9顶点）及多种极值图族）上测试，辅以主动搜索算法寻找反例。最终幸存猜想通过确定性翻译为Lean 4语句骨架，并由两个神经定理证明器（DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B和OProver-32B）生成候选证明，经mathlib4内核验证。

Result: 在HPC集群上运行多轮后，系统产出6,522条通过反驳数据集、新颖性过滤器和主动搜索的猜想，其中包括湮灭数与边覆盖数之间的非平凡关系（针对二分图和正则图），已手工证明成立。完整流水线已实现端到端并通过初步测试，当前在集群上持续运行。

Conclusion: AutoGraphForge展示了将机器学习引导的猜想发现、大规模计算反驳与基于Lean 4的形式化验证统一于一体的可行性，标志着向全自动、可信的数学发现系统迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: We report on our ongoing project to develop a computational pipeline, AutoGraphForge, for an automated graph-theoretic conjecturing-refuting-formalizing-proving system. Conjecture generation is counterexample-guided and runs in rounds: a Graffiti3 generator proposes conjectures over a small, evolving snapshot table $T$ (initially a few hundred graphs with their computed invariants) that grows only by counterexamples to its own conjectures. A novelty filter of $559$ classical and folklore relations, closed under transitive composition and linear identity substitution, decides via a linear program whether a candidate is already implied by known results. Surviving candidates are tested against a dataset of about $348,000$ graphs, unioning the complete House of Graphs invariant export, the exhaustive census of all connected graphs on at most nine vertices, several extremal families (strongly regular, minimal Ramsey, Cayley, cages, barbells, lollipops, spiders), and random models. Counterexample-search algorithms then attack the remainder. Run for several rounds on an HPC cluster, the loop yields $6,522$ conjectures that survived the refutation dataset, the novelty filter and every active-search run -- among them nontrivial relations between the annihilation number and the edge-cover number for bipartite and regular graphs, which we prove by hand. A subsequent formalization and proving stage deterministically translates each surviving conjecture into a Lean 4 statement skeleton; every candidate proof is kernel-verified against a pinned mathlib4 and our custom invariant preamble. This stage integrates two neural provers -- DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B (served with vLLM) and the Lean-specialised OProver-32B -- behind the independent kernel check. It is implemented end-to-end and passes initial sanity checks, with the full pipeline currently running on the cluster.

</details>


### [189] [Making Every Tool Call Count: Necessary Tool-Evidence Path Rewards for Agentic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03493)
*Xingming Long,Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Hanqi Feng,Shaojie Zhang,Barnabas Poczos,Chao Jiang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NTEP（必要工具-证据路径）标注方案及NTEP-R奖励机制，通过细粒度监督工具调用的意图与证据提取过程，解决智能体VLM在工具使用中证据获取不足的问题。基于此训练的8B参数模型NTEP-8B在七个图像基准上显著提升了搜索准确率和工具使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体VLM的训练主要基于最终答案正确性进行评估，导致工具使用过程缺乏充分监督，模型常发出冗余或偏离目标的工具调用，即便调用正确工具也往往无法从观察结果中提取必要信息。

Method: 提出NTEP标注方案，显式指定每个查询所需的外部证据及对应工具调用；在此基础上提出NTEP-R奖励机制，奖励模型调用前意图与必要证据获取目标对齐、调用后观察总结与必要证据对齐，并引入非重复目标正则化项以惩罚重复调用。

Result: 在七个图像基准上的实验表明，8B参数的NTEP-8B在统一三工具框架内显著提升了面向搜索的准确率和工具使用效率。

Conclusion: 细粒度的工具-证据路径监督对于训练鲁棒的智能体VLM至关重要，不仅改善最终答案正确性，还能提升工具调用的效率和有效性。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) can directly answer many image-grounded questions, yet they often struggle with complex queries requiring fine-grained visual details or external knowledge. To acquire this missing evidence, agentic VLMs invoke tools such as image cropping, image search, and text search. However, existing training paradigms primarily evaluate tool-use based on final answer correctness, leaving evidence acquisition and utilization insufficiently supervised. This leads to two critical shortcomings: (i) models frequently issue redundant or off-target tool calls that fail to gather necessary evidence, and (ii) even when appropriate tools are called, models often fail to extract the necessary information from the resulting observations. To address these limitations, we introduce the NTEP (Necessary Tool-Evidence Path), a novel annotation scheme that explicitly specifies the essential external evidence and corresponding tool calls for each query. Building upon this, we propose NTEP-R (NTEP Reward), a supervision mechanism ensuring that each tool invocation strictly advances the reasoning process toward the final solution. Specifically, our approach rewards the agent for aligning its pre-call intent with a necessary evidence-seeking goal, and for ensuring the information summarized from the post-call observation aligns with the necessary evidence. Furthermore, we introduce a non-repeated-goal regularizer to penalize redundant calls that revisit satisfied NTEP goals. Extensive evaluations on seven image-grounded benchmarks demonstrate that our 8B-parameter instantiation, NTEP-8B, significantly improves both search-oriented accuracy and tool-use efficiency within a unified three-tool framework. These results highlight the critical value of fine-grained tool-evidence path supervision for training robust agentic VLMs.

</details>


### [190] [GrowPage: On-Demand KV Budgeting for Efficient LLM Reasoning Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03494)
*Qiankun Ma,Yanjiang Zhou,Zinan Xiong,Haofei Wang,Zhen Song,Yang Xiang,Ziyao Zhang,Hairong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GrowPage是一个按需KV缓存预算框架，将KV容量视为运行时资源，通过双时间尺度查询摘要动态估计注意力需求变化，在容量边界时压缩现有KV或获取额外物理页，从而在推理场景中实现更好的性能-吞吐量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输出推理使KV缓存成为LLM高效服务的严重内存瓶颈。现有KV压缩方法依赖预定义的每请求预算，仅调整保留哪些KV状态，总容量在解码过程中固定不变，无法适应推理工作负载中不同请求间以及单个请求生成过程中显著变化的KV容量需求。

Method: GrowPage维护轻量级双时间尺度查询摘要，分别捕捉近期和长期注意力行为，利用它们的相对注意力工作集来估计需求演变。在每个容量边界处，GrowPage在当前分配内压缩KV状态，或当出现更广泛需求时获取额外的物理页。同时与PagedAttention的页级内存抽象集成，保持连续批处理和前缀缓存能力。

Result: 在多个模型上的推理基准实验中，GrowPage相比现有方法实现了更优的性能-吞吐量权衡。

Conclusion: 通过将KV容量从静态预算转变为动态可扩展的运行时资源，GrowPage能够适应推理过程中注意力需求的变化，在保持连续批处理和前缀缓存兼容性的同时，显著改善了推理服务的效率。

Abstract: Long-output reasoning has made the key--value (KV) cache a critical memory bottleneck for efficient LLM serving. Existing KV compression methods usually rely on a predefined per-request budget and adjust only which KV states are retained, leaving the total capacity fixed throughout decoding. However, reasoning workloads exhibit substantial demand variation: different requests require different KV capacities, and the attention demand of an individual request evolves during generation. We introduce \textbf{GrowPage}, an on-demand KV budgeting framework that treats KV capacity as a runtime resource. GrowPage maintains lightweight dual-timescale query summaries to capture recent and long-term attention behaviors, and uses their relative attention working sets to estimate demand evolution. At each capacity boundary, GrowPage either compresses KV states within the current allocation or acquires an additional physical page when broader demand emerges. By integrating with PagedAttention's page-level memory abstraction, GrowPage preserves continuous batching and prefix caching. Experiments on reasoning benchmarks across multiple models show that GrowPage achieves a superior performance--throughput trade-off over existing approaches.

</details>


### [191] [PPO-STGNN: A Proximal Policy Optimization Approach with Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks for DAG Task Scheduling in Cloud-Edge-End Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03503)
*Yangshuo Qi,Chenwei Wang,Zihan Shen,Songlin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PPO-STGNN算法，将近端策略优化（PPO）与时空图神经网络（STGNN）结合，用于云-边-端协同环境中DAG任务调度。该方法通过STGNN提取DAG任务拓扑和物理资源图的特征，利用PPO优化调度策略以最小化makespan和调度长度比（SLR），并提高CPU和内存负载均衡。为加速收敛引入多教师行为克隆预训练机制。实验证明该方法在动态异构云-边-端DAG调度场景中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网迅速发展，云-边-端协同环境中的计算密集型DAG任务日益普遍，但各节点在计算能力、网络带宽和能耗方面高度异构，使得具有复杂依赖关系的任务高效调度成为NP难问题。传统启发式算法和常规强化学习方法难以捕捉系统资源的时空动态特性，亟需更有效的调度方案。

Method: 提出PPO-STGNN算法：(1) 利用STGNN从DAG任务拓扑和物理云-边-端资源图中提取时空特征；(2) 通过PPO算法优化调度策略，以最小化makespan和SLR为目标，同时改善CPU和内存负载均衡；(3) 引入多教师行为克隆机制进行预训练以加速收敛。

Result: 实验结果表明，PPO-STGNN在保持较低任务完成时间的同时显著提升了负载均衡性能，适用于动态异构的云-边-端DAG调度场景。

Conclusion: PPO-STGNN结合了深度强化学习与图神经网络的各自优势，有效解决了云-边-端异构环境中DAG任务调度的时空动态捕捉难题，并兼顾了完成时间与负载均衡的双重优化目标。

Abstract: With the rapid development of the Internet of Things, computation intensive directed acyclic graph (DAG) tasks have become increasingly common in cloud-edge-end collaborative environments. However, cloud, edge, and end nodes are highly heterogeneous in computing capacity, network bandwidth, and energy consumption, which makes the efficient scheduling of tasks with complex dependencies an NP-hard problem. Traditional heuristic algorithms and conventional reinforcement-learning methods often fail to capture the spatio-temporal dynamics of system resources. This paper proposes PPO-STGNN, a DAG task-scheduling algorithm that integrates proximal policy optimization (PPO) with spatio-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs). The method uses an STGNN to extract features from both the DAG task topology and the physical cloud-edge-end resource graph, and then optimizes the scheduling policy through PPO to minimize makespan and schedule length ratio (SLR) while improving CPU and memory load balancing. To accelerate convergence, a multi-teacher behavior-cloning mechanism is introduced for pretraining. Experimental results show that PPO-STGNN significantly improves load balancing while maintaining a low completion time, making it suitable for dynamic and heterogeneous cloud-edge- end DAG scheduling scenarios.

</details>


### [192] [What Matters for Aggressive Decoding-Time KV Eviction? Temporal Aggregation and Ranking Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03515)
*Bo Zeng,Yu Zhao,Yefeng Liu,Zhihong Lu,Xuanfan Ni,Xintong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究解码时KV缓存压缩中时间聚合规则（EMA）的重要性，提出InertiaKV及其惰性变体InertiaKV-Lazy，并探索了Score-Free解码这一独立运行点，揭示了时间聚合与排序保持是独立且重要的设计因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩研究过度聚焦于token打分函数的设计，而跨解码步聚合打分的时间规则被视为实现细节。作者发现在激进压缩下EMA聚合使近似保序的打分修改难以区分，说明需要重新审视聚合规则的作用。

Method: 提出InertiaKV（基于EMA的解码时驱逐方法）及其周期刷新变体InertiaKV-Lazy；同时提出Score-Free解码：在首个解码步对完整上下文打分一次、冻结排序并去除后续所有打分；在六个开源骨干模型上结合LongBench、LongBench-v2和RULER基准进行评估，分析不同打分器与聚合规则在驱逐集层面的差异。

Result: Value-norm和entropy变体与attention高度相关、保留集几乎不变；KeyDiff、key norm、recency及学习型打分器改变排序导致质量下降。InertiaKV-Lazy相比全刷新InertiaKV获得1.34-1.46x解码吞吐提升；Score-Free解码平均质量变化仅+0.03。

Conclusion: 时间聚合与排序保持是独立且重要的KV压缩设计因素；该结果不意味着打分质量总体无关，而是揭示在EMA聚合下许多打分改进被掩蔽。

Abstract: Decoding-time KV cache compression research focuses heavily on designing better token scoring functions, while the temporal rule that aggregates scores across decode steps is often treated as an implementation detail. Under aggressive KV compression, we find that exponential-moving-average (EMA) aggregation makes approximately order-preserving scorer modifications largely indistinguishable at the eviction-set level. Value-norm and entropy variants remain highly correlated with attention and produce nearly unchanged retention sets, whereas KeyDiff, key norm, recency, and a learned scorer alter the ranking and degrade substantially. We associate this stability with the evaluated aggregation, which couples layer weighting and temporal retention. Building on this observation, we introduce InertiaKV, an EMA-based decoding-time eviction method, and InertiaKV-Lazy, its periodic-refresh variant, which yields 1.34-1.46x decode throughput relative to full refresh InertiaKV. We also study Score-Free decoding as a separate empirical operating point: it scores the full context once at the first decode step, freezes that ranking, and incurs an average quality change of +0.03 while removing all subsequent scoring. Across six open-weight backbones and the LongBench, LongBench-v2, and RULER benchmarks, the results identify temporal aggregation and ranking preservation as distinct, consequential design factors; they do not imply that scoring quality is irrelevant in general.

</details>


### [193] [CulturalMenuBench: Probing the Knowledge-Application Gap in Multimodal Culinary Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03526)
*Bo Zeng,Linfeng Gao,Peiqin Lin,Yu Zhao,Mingyan Zeng,Yu Tong,Xintong Wang,Linlong Xu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Qinggang Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CulturalMenuBench基准（18个地区、10种语言、4,870个菜品的10项任务），用于区分多模态语言模型在食品识别上的高分是源于真正的文化理解还是简单的视觉匹配。实验发现12个模型在标准选择题上可超过94%准确率，但在将菜品归属到中国地方菜系的任务中最高仅56%，且错误模式与随机猜测一致，说明模型具备相关知识但无法通过视觉输入激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型在食品识别基准上已达到接近满分的成绩，但作者质疑这种成功是否真正反映了文化理解，而非仅是视觉匹配。现有基准无法区分这两种能力，因此需要设计一个能揭示模型是否有能力把视觉信息与程序性/文化性知识联系起来的新评估框架。

Method: 构建CulturalMenuBench基准，将成品菜品图片和逐步烹饪图片与配料、程序文本及地区标签配对，涵盖从基础识别到基于过程的文化归因的10项任务。对12个模型进行系统评估，并通过消融实验（移除顺序烹饪图像）和诊断分析（错误模式、视觉显著性与文化结构的关联、名称与图像判别对比）来揭示知识应用差距的内在机理。

Result: 标准多项选择任务上模型可超过94%准确率，但在中国地方菜系归因任务（相同四选格式）上最高仅56%；错误模式与随机猜测一致；准确率随视觉显著性变化而非文化结构；仅凭菜名分类比借助图像更准确（高出7-18个百分点）。消融实验证实过程性任务确实需要程序性证据。

Conclusion: 近乎完美的食品识别表现可能掩盖模型无法应用文化知识的根本缺陷。研究呼吁训练过程应明确地将感知、程序和文化背景相连接，CulturalMenuBench可作为评估此类能力的有效工具。

Abstract: Multimodal language models achieve near-ceiling scores on food recognition benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether this success reflects genuine cultural understanding or mere visual matching. To probe this distinction, we introduce CulturalMenuBench, a benchmark of 4,870 items in 10 languages across 18 regions; its 10 tasks pair final-dish and step-by-step cooking images with ingredients, procedural text, and regional labels, spanning basic recognition to process-grounded cultural attribution. Evaluating 12 models exposes a substantial knowledge-application gap: models exceeding 94% on standard multiple-choice tasks drop to at most 56% when attributing dishes to Chinese regional cuisines, despite an identical four-way format. Diagnostic analyses explain why: error patterns are consistent with random guessing, accuracy tracks visual distinctiveness rather than cultural structure, and models classify cuisines more accurately from dish names alone than from images (+7-18 points). The knowledge is thus present but cannot be activated through visual input. An ablation confirms these tasks genuinely require procedural evidence: removing sequential cooking images selectively degrades process-grounded tasks while others remain stable. Overall, CulturalMenuBench shows that near-perfect recognition can conceal an inability to apply cultural knowledge, motivating training that explicitly connects perception, procedure, and cultural context. Code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [194] [NeoRed: A Knowledge-Logic-Alignment Multimodal Large Language Model for Neonatal Respiratory Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03527)
*Yinan Liu,Hongtai Xia,Haoran Xu,Jiankang Hong,Jingkuan Song,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出面向新生儿呼吸系统疾病的专用多模态大语言模型NeoRed，通过知识先验注入、诊断逻辑约束和视觉语义对齐三类机制，实现异质临床上下文与胸部X光片的联合诊断与报告生成，在新生儿数据集上优于现有MLLMs，同时在成人基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿呼吸系统疾病是新生儿发病率和死亡率的主要原因，临床实践面临重大挑战。现有MLLMs在新生儿诊断中存在两大局限：(1) 训练数据以成人为主导致的领域鸿沟；(2) 对多维度临床上下文的整合不足，难以支撑准确诊断。现有模型无法直接满足新生儿诊断报告生成的临床需求。

Method: 提出NeoRed，首个面向新生儿呼吸系统疾病的多模态大语言模型。具体包含：构建两个真实临床数据集NeoCXR和NeoCXR-EV；设计知识-逻辑-对齐（KLA）框架，从三个维度约束模型行为：1) 知识先验注入（KPI）将新生儿科专家诊断先验融入多模态表征，引导跨模态疾病特异性注意力；2) 诊断逻辑约束（DLC）将生成报告语义与多模态诊断逻辑对齐；3) 视觉语义对齐（VSA）建立视觉特征与影像结论之间的语义对应。数据集将开放申请获取。

Result: NeoRed在NeoCXR上实现ROUGE-L达53.29%，临床效能F1分数达65.19%，优于现有MLLMs；在成人基准（MIMIC-CXR和IU-Xray）上保持竞争力的报告生成性能。

Conclusion: NeoRed成功弥补了新生儿诊断报告生成领域的空白，证明结合领域知识先验、诊断逻辑约束和视觉语义对齐的KLA框架能够有效提升新生儿呼吸系统疾病的联合诊断能力，同时具备跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Neonatal respiratory diseases are a major cause of neonatal morbidity and mortality, posing substantial challenges in clinical practice. Despite recent advances, existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face two key limitations in neonatal diagnosis: (1) domain gap arising from predominantly adult training data; (2) insufficient integration of multidimensional clinical context for accurate diagnosis. To address these challenges, we collect two real-world clinical datasets (NeoCXR and NeoCXR-EV) and propose NeoRed, to the best of our knowledge, the first MLLM tailored for neonatal respiratory disease, filling the gap in neonatal diagnostic reports generation. To enhance joint diagnosis from heterogeneous clinical context and chest X-rays, we design a novel Knowledge-Logic-Alignment (KLA) framework which constrains model behavior from three perspectives: 1) Knowledge Prior Injection (KPI) incorporates neonatologist-inspired diagnostic priors into multimodal representations, guiding disease-specific attention across modalities; 2) Diagnostic Logic Constraint (DLC) aligns the semantics of generated reports with multimodal diagnostic logic; and 3) Visual Semantic Alignment (VSA) establishes semantic correspondence between visual features and imaging conclusions.
